2.83Z\\\"\\u003e\\u003c/path\\u003e\\u003c/svg\\u003e\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/div\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eScholarNexus is a full-stack academic research assistant that aggregates results from 9 major scholarly APIs in a single query. Instead of jumping between Google Scholar, arXiv, PubMed, and IEEE, you describe your research goal once in natural language — the platform understands the intent, expands the query into multiple search variants, fans them out in parallel, deduplicates the merged results, and ranks them by a composite score combining citation count, recency, source authority, and open-access preference.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003eEvery result comes with an AI-generated summary, key contributions, methodology notes, and limitations. class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Overview</h2><a id=\"user-content-overview\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Overview\" href=\"#overview\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ScholarNexus is a full-stack academic research assistant that aggregates results from 9 major scholarly APIs in a single query. Instead of jumping between Google Scholar, arXiv, PubMed, and IEEE, you describe your research goal once in natural language — the platform understands the intent, expands the query into multiple search variants, fans them out in parallel, deduplicates the merged results, and ranks them by a composite score combining citation count, recency, source authority, and open-access preference.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Every result comes with an AI-generated summary, key contributions, methodology notes, and limitations. Beyond search, the platform supports citation-network visualization, evidence synthesis across multiple papers, conversational Q&amp;A on uploaded PDFs, persistent collections, search alerts, and full-text exports in BibTeX/RIS/CSV.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">What problem does it solve?</h3><a id=\"user-content-what-problem-does-it-solve\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What problem does it solve?\" href=\"#what-problem-does-it-solve\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Researchers waste significant time switching between siloed academic databases, each with its own query syntax, ranking algorithm, and metadata quality. ScholarNexus eliminates this friction by:</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Unifying 9 sources</strong> behind a single natural-language interface.</li>\n<li><strong>Removing duplicates</strong> that appear across arXiv, Crossref, Semantic Scholar, etc.</li>\n<li><strong>Ranking intelligently</strong> — not just by citation count, but by a transparent composite score.</li>\n<li><strong>Synthesizing</strong> findings across multiple papers using AI.</li>\n<li><strong>Persisting</strong> your library, collections, alerts, and Q&amp;A history locally.</li>\n</ol>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Key Features</h2><a id=\"user-content-key-features\" .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 queries</strong></td>\n<td>Ask \"papers on graph neural networks for cyberattack detection\" — the AI rewrites it into precise search variants.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>9 parallel sources</strong></td>\n<td>Semantic Scholar, arXiv, Crossref, PubMed, OpenAlex, IEEE Xplore, bioRxiv, Europe PMC, CORE.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Agentic query expansion</strong></td>\n<td>The AI generates up to 3 query variants to widen recall without sacrificing precision.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Intelligent deduplication</strong></td>\n<td>Papers returned by multiple sources are merged using DOI, title-hash, and fuzzy matching.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Composite ranking</strong></td>\n<td>Score = <code>f(citations, recency, sourceAuthority, openAccess)</code>.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Filters</strong></td>\n<td>Year range, open-access-only, min citations, source filter, sort order.</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">AI-Powered Insights</h3><a viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">ScholarNexus includes several built-in security measures:</p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Layer</th>\n<th>Implementation</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Rate limiting</strong></td>\n<td>In-memory token bucket per IP for all API routes (<code>src/lib/security.ts</code>).</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>SSRF protection</strong></td>\n<td>Outbound URLs are validated against private IP ranges before fetch.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Input sanitization</strong></td>\n<td>All user input is sanitized and validated with Zod schemas.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Secret hygiene</strong></td>\n<td><code>.env</code> is gitignored; <code>.env.example</code> contains only placeholder values.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>No DB credentials in repo</strong></td>\n<td><code>DATABASE_URL</code> is read from environment at runtime.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>No client-side secrets</strong></td>\n<td>All API keys (IEEE, CORE) are server-side only.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>SQL injection</strong></td>\n<td>Prisma's parameterized queries prevent SQL injection.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>XSS</strong></td>\n<td>React's automatic escaping + Zod validation on all inputs.</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Reporting a vulnerability</h3><a id=\"user-content-reporting-a-vulnerability\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Reporting a vulnerability\" href=\"#reporting-a-vulnerability\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 issue, <strong>please do not open a public issue</strong>. Instead, email the maintainer directly or open a private security advisory on GitHub.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Contributing</h2><a id=\"user-content-contributing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Contributing\" href=\"#contributing\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Contributions are welcome!
